公司年度财务报告已经中兴华会计师事务所（特殊普通合伙）根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》审计，并出具了标准无保留意见的审计报告。

本年度报告及摘要中比较列报的以前年度财务数据已经中兴华会计师事务所（特殊普通合伙）根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》进行审计调整。

本年度报告中的财务数据为合并报表经审计后的数据，资本相关数据及主要经营指标按照监管要求合并报表口径计算得出。

本年度报告中注册地址及网点地址按照济南莱芜区划调整后的情况列示。

公司董事长岳隆杰、行长朱鸣以及财务负责人刘海新，保证年度报告中财务报告的真实、完整。

# IMPORTANT NOTES

The Board of Directors, the directors and senior management of the Company undertake that there are no misrepresentation, misleading statement, or material omission in the Report and its summary, and accept joint and several liability for the authenticity, accuracy and completeness of the contents of the Report.

The annual financial report of the company has been audited by Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP according to the Accounting Standards for Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance and a standard unqualified auditor's opinion has been issued.

The financial data of previous years listed in this annual report and its summary for comparison have been audited and adjusted by Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP according to the Accounting Standards for Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance.

The financial data listed in this annual report is the audited data of consolidated statements and capital related data has been calculated upon the standard of consolidated statements in accordance with the regulatory requirements.

The registered address and branch address in this annual report are listed in accordance with those after adjustment of the administrative division Laiwu City and Jinan City.

Chairman Yue Longjie, President Zhu Ming and Finance Director Liu Haixin confirm the authenticity and completeness of the financial statements in the Annual Report.

Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

## 七、公司各类风险和风险管理情况

公司全面风险管理体系由董事会、监事会、高级管理层、全面风险管理部门、风险管理主控部门、风险管理责任部门、内审部门及各分支机构构成。公司董事会负责建立和保持有效的全面风险管理体系，承担风险管理的最终责任。高级管理层负责制定风险管理政策和程序，承担全面风险管理的实施责任。高级管理层下设信用风险审查委员会、资产负债管理委员会等专业委员会，并指定各类风险主控部门及管理部门。2018年，公司始终坚持依法合规经营理念，遵循“风险与收益匹配”原则，不断完善全面风险管理体系，持续优化风险管理制度，扎实推动风险管理措施落地实施，风险管理涵盖所有业务和操作环节，全面风险管理水平显著提升。报告期内，公司面临的主要风险有信用风险、市场风险、操作风险、流动性风险、信息科技风险等。

## (一) 信用风险及对策

信用风险是指债务人或交易对手未能履行合同规定的义务或信用质量发生变化，影响金融产品价值，从而给本行造成经济损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强信用风险管理：一是强化差异化政策引导。制定年度授信政策指引，建立健全新旧动能转换、绿色产业等客户沙盘，实现信贷资源精准投放。二是优化审查审批流程。通过完善评级授信调查报告模板、推行授信延伸审查及限时办结等措施，严把授信准入关口，强化授信预警管理，有效提升授信审批质效。三是规范信贷出账管理。全面推行放款中心模式，所有信贷出账均由相对独立的放款中心进行审核放款，强化授信条件落实。四是强化授信业务后评价。从授信政策执行、授信审查审批、授信资料质量等角度入手，全面开展授信业务后评价工作，督促问题单位立查立改，全面提升授信业务质量。五是深入开展“资产质量攻坚”行动。召开专题推进会，实施不良信贷资产处置责任制，成立分行专职清收团队，逐户制定非应计贷款处置的时间表，扎实推动不良资产清收质效提升。

## (二) 市场风险及对策

市场风险是指因市场价格（利率、汇率、股票价格和商品价格）的不利变动，从而使银行表内外业务发生损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强市场风险管理：一是完善市场风险管理体系。建立与业务性质、规模、复杂程度相适应的市场风险管理体系，明确“两会一层”、市场风险主控部门与风险承担部门的职责，制定清晰的市场风险管理政策和程序，优化识别、计量、监测及评估市场风险的工具和方法。二是加大市场风险监测分析。建立完善市场风险报告体系，逐日监控市场风险限额执行、信用风险违约等情况，并定期向高级管理层及相关部门报告市场风险管理状况。同时每日监测限额指标运行，根据监测及时进行风险预警，并要求相关部门研究解决方案。三是强化限额管理。制定

债券业务风险限额方案，明确投资业务的交易限额、风险限额及止损限额，有效规范债券投资业务。四是优化投资策略和资产配置结构。积极调整金融市场业务投资策略，适当压降风险资产规模，有效降低市场风险影响。五是夯实外汇业务管理基础。制定外汇资金管理办法等制度，明确资金交易员日常操作权限，强化交易纪律和额度管控。

## (三)操作风险及对策

操作风险是指由不完善或有问题的内部控制程序、员工、信息科技系统以及外部事件所造成损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强操作风险管理：一是深入推进“基础管理提升年”各项工作。将2018年确定为“基础管理提升年”，制定实施方案和工作配档表，着力解决发展中的基础管理薄弱环节，进一步提升效率、夯实内控、防范风险，实现各项业务合规、高效开展。二是组织开展合规检查和风险排查工作。制定年度专项风险排查方案，在全行范围内组织开展市场乱象整治、票据业务检查等。针对检查排查发现的问题，从管理、制度、执行等方面进行系统性落实整改，进一步堵塞管理与执行漏洞，预防潜在风险隐患。三是建立内控合规与操作风险管理平台。上线运行内控合规与操作风险管理系统，配套梳理业务管理流程、风险点及控制措施等，实现内控合规与操作风险管理资源有效整合。四是强化员工行为管理。持续开展员工行为排查，通过征信排查、授信公示、家访等方式，全面排查员工参与民间借贷等异常行为，进一步增强全员红线意识。五是认真落实案件防控工作要求。印发年度案防工作指导意见，明确案防工作重点领域和关键环节，扎实开展交流轮岗、离任审计、强制休假等工作。

## (四)流动性风险及对策

流动性风险是指本行无法以合理成本及时获得充足资金，用于偿付到期债务、履行其他支付义务和满足正常业务开展的其他资金需求的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强流动性风险管理：一是完善流动性风险管理制度体系。梳理、诊断公司流动性风险管控流程，修订完善流动性风险管理办法等制度，设定合理的流动性风险限额并强化监测报告，确保流动性需求持续得到保障。二是强化流动性指标预警分析。加大备付金比率、流动性比例、流动性缺口率等指标监测分析，及时识别流动性风险隐患，动态计量流动性风险，并及时采取风险缓释措施，有效防范流动性风险。三是丰富流动性风险管理工具。优化升级资产负债管理系统，进一步提升流动性风险的识别、计量和监测能力，完善现金流模型和流动性压力测试方案，科学预测未来的现金流。四是调整优化负债结构。加强主动负债管理，积极拓宽资金来源，对全行流动性形成有力支撑。同时，通过调整FTP定价策略，引导分支机构积极拓展稳定性负债，促使负债稳定性进一步提升。五是定期开展压力测试。通过开展压力测试，测算不同压力情景下流动性管理可能面临的风险因素，并对流动性应急预案进行修正，确保流动性触发应急预案时风险能够得到有效处置和控制。

## （五）信息科技风险及对策

信息科技风险是指信息科技在银行运用过程中，由于自然因素、人为因素、技术漏洞和管理缺陷产生的操作、法律和声誉等风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强信息科技风险管理：一是加大信息科技治理力度。组织召开三次信息科技管理委员会会议，修订完善信息系统突发事件应急管理办法等制度29项，健全信息科技风险管理流程框架。二是开展信息科技风险排查。通过印发信息安全风险提示、开展基础软硬件产品缺陷信息协查等工作，全面排查信息科技风险隐患，并进行系统性整改落实，进一步提升信息科技风险管控能力。三是强化网络风险监测。部署总行互联网行为审计系统和分支行互联网认证审计系统，实现互联网的微信认证和审计。组织无线网络安全管理专项自查和风险评估，开展无线网络渗透测试，提升公司物理网络安全。四是加强信息科技业务连续性管理。组织开展数据中心机房市电整体切换演练、同城清算系统突发事件应急演练等各类应急演练40余次，进一步提升各类业务连续性。五是增强客户信息保护力度。建立客户信息保护工作台账，梳理生产系统敏感信息，完成批量导出报表敏感字段屏蔽工作，建设企业网盘，强化客户信息保护。

## 八、内部控制制度的完整性、合理性与有效性

公司高度重视内控制度建设，秉承全面、审慎、有效原则，不断健全内控管理体系，确保各类内控制度流程科学、严密，保障公司高质量稳健发展。报告期内，公司制定深化制度建设工作方案，在全行范围内组织开展制度宣讲培训及竞赛工作，增强全员对制度的敬畏意识和执行能力。同时，根据国家政策和公司发展需要不断改进和完善内控制度体系，将合规管理要求及规章制度映射到各项经营活动、各业务条线、各岗位员工。报告期内，公司制度评审委员会组织召开评审会议11次，审议新建制度38项。通过对内部控制体系建设、实施和运行结果开展系统性调查、测试、分析和评估，公司内部制度体系基本涵盖了全行所有产品、渠道、交易、管理及其关键环节，流程设置科学合理，在规范财务会计行为、提高会计信息质量、强化经营管理、控制经营风险、堵塞漏洞、防止舞弊等方面得到了有效的执行。

## VII. Risks of the Company and risk management

The comprehensive risk management system of the Company consists of the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors, the senior management, the comprehensive risk management department, the main control department for risk management, the responsible department for risk management, the internal audit department, and all Branches. The Board of Directors is responsible for establishing and maintaining an effective and comprehensive risk management system and ultimately for risk management. The senior management is responsible for formulating the risk management policies and procedures and for implementing comprehensive risk management. The senior management has set up special committees such as the Credit Risk Review Committee and the Assets-Liabilities Management Committee, and also designated the main control departments and management departments of various risks. In 2018, the Company always stuck to the operating concept of legal compliance, followed the principle of "matching risks and returns", constantly improved the comprehensive risk management system, continuously optimized the risk management systems, made solid efforts to promote the implementation of risk management measures, and spread risk management to cover all business and operational links, so that the comprehensive risk management level has been significantly improved. During the reporting period, the main risks faced by the Company include credit risk, market risk, operational risk, liquidity risk, IT risk, and etc.

##### (i) Credit risks and their countermeasures

Credit risk refers to the risks of economic losses to the Company caused by the influence on the value of financial products resulted from failure to fulfil the obligations stipulated in the contract of or changes of the credit quality of debtors or counterparties. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened the credit risk management from the following aspects. Firstly, the Company enhanced the guiding role of the differentiated policies, by defining the annual guidelines of the credit granting policies, establishing and perfecting client sand tables for old and new capacity conversion, green industries and so on, and realizing accurate release of credit resources. Secondly, the Company optimized the review and approval procedures. By improving the survey report template for credit rating and credit extension, implementing the review and closing within a certain time limit for credit extension, and other measures, the Company strictly controlled the access to credit, strengthened the early warning management for credit granting, and effectively improved the quality and efficiency of credit approval. Thirdly, the Company standardized the management of credit payment. A central mode for loan payment was fully implemented so that all credits were released by the relatively independent loan center after examination and review, and the conditions of credit granting were strengthened to implement. Fourthly, the Company strengthened the post-evaluation of credit granting business. Starting from the perspectives of the implementation of the credit granting policies, examination and approval of credit granting, quality of credit granting data, and etc., the Company comprehensively carried out the post-evaluation of credit granting business, urged the units which were found with problems to conduct investigation and rectification in a timely manner, so as to overall improve the quality of credit granting business. Fifthly, the Company deepened the efforts to improve the asset quality, convening a thematic promotion meeting, implementing the responsibility system for the disposal of non-performing credit assets, setting up the full-time team for settlement and collection, and preparing the schedules for the disposal of non-accrued loans one by one, so as to promote the quality and efficiency of the settlement and collection of non-performing assets.

##### (ii) Market risks and their countermeasures

Market risk refers to the risks of losses of in-balance-sheet and off-balance-sheet businesses due to adverse changes in market price (interest rate, exchange rate, stock price and commodity price). During the reporting period, the Company strengthened the market risk management from the following aspects. Firstly, the Company perfected the market risk management system by establishing the market risk management system which matches with the nature, scale and complexity of business, making clear the responsibilities of "two boards and one level (the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors, and the

management)", and of main control and responsible departments of market risks, formulating the clear market risk management policies and procedures, and optimizing the tools and methods to recognize, measure, monitor and evaluate market risks. Secondly, the Company strengthened the monitoring and analysis of market risks by establishing and improving the market risk reporting system, monitoring the implementation of market risk limits and credit risk defaults on a daily basis, and regularly reporting the market risk management status to the senior management and relevant departments. At the same time, the limit index operation was monitored on a daily basis, risk pre-warning was carried out timely according to the monitoring results, and relevant departments were asked to develop solutions. Thirdly, the Company strengthened the limit management by formulating a risk limit scheme for bond business, clearly specifying trading limit, risk limit and loss-stopping limit for investment business, and effectively standardizing the bond investment business. Fourthly, the Company optimized the investment strategy and asset allocation structure, by actively adjusting the investment strategies for financial market businesses, appropriately reducing the scale of risky assets, and effectively eliminating the impact of market risks. Fifthly, the Company strengthened the foundation for managing foreign exchange businesses, by formulating the rules and regulations such as the Management Measures for Foreign Exchange Funds, clarifying the daily operation authority of fund traders, and strengthening the trading discipline and limit control.

##### (iii) Operational risks and their countermeasures

Operational risk refers to the risks of loss caused by imperfect or problematic internal control procedures, employees, IT systems, or external events. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened the operational risk management from the following aspects. Firstly, the Company deeply implemented the various works of the "Year of Basic Management Improvement", by setting 2018 as the "Year of Basic Management Improvement", formulating the implementation plan and work allocation table, focusing on solving the weak links of basic management during the development, further improving the efficiency, strengthening the internal control, and preventing risks, so as to achieve the compliance and efficient development of various businesses. Secondly, the Company organized and carried out the compliance inspections and risk investigation and screening, by formulating the annual special risk investigation and screening plan, organizing and carrying out the rectification and control of market chaos and inspection of bill businesses within the whole Bank, carrying out systematic rectification from the aspects of management, system and execution based on all the problems found in the inspections, so as to further fill up the gaps in management and execution and prevent potential risks and hidden dangers. Thirdly, the Company established and put online the internal control compliance and operational risk management platform, and accordingly sorting the business management process, risk points, control measures and so on to realize the effective integration of the resources for internal control & compliance and operational risk management. Fourthly, the Company strengthened the management of employee behavior, by continuously inspecting and screening employee behavior, overall investing and screening the abnormal behavior of employees such as participation in private lending through credit investigation and screening, credit publicity, home visits and other means, so as to further enhance the red line awareness of all staff. Fifthly, the Company earnestly implemented the work requirements of case prevention and control, by issuing the annual guidelines on case prevention, clearly identifying important areas and key links in the work of case prevention, and carrying out exchanges, job rotation, off-office auditing, and mandatory vacations in a solid manner.

##### (iv) Liquidity risks and their countermeasures

Liquidity risk refers to the risks of inability to timely obtain sufficient funds at a reasonable cost for the purpose of repaying due debts, fulfilling other payment obligations or meeting other needs for funds used for normal business. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened the liquidity risk management from the following aspects. Firstly, the Company improved the liquidity risk management system, by sorting out and diagnosing the liquidity risk management and control process, revising and improving the liquidity risk management methods and other systems, setting reasonable liquidity risk limits and strengthening monitoring reports, so as to ensure that liquidity demands are continuously guaranteed. Secondly, the Company strengthened the early warning analysis of liquidity indexes, by

increasing the monitoring and analysis of reserve ratio, liquidity ratio, liquidity gap ratio and other indexes, timely identifying potential liquidity risks, dynamically measuring liquidity risks, and taking timely risk mitigation measures, so as to effectively prevent liquidity risks. Thirdly, the Company enriched the liquidity risk management tools, by optimizing and upgrading the management system of assets and liabilities, further improving the ability to identify, measure and monitor liquidity risks, perfecting the cash flow model and testing scheme of liquidity stress, and scientifically predicting the future cash flow. Fourthly, the Company adjusted and optimized the debt structure, by strengthening active liability management and expanding sources of funds, so as to provide strong support for the liquidity of the whole Bank. At the same time, the Company guided the Branches to actively expand the stable debts by adjusting the FTP pricing strategy, so as to promote the stability of liabilities. Fifthly, the Company carried out regular stress tests so as to measure and calculate the risk factors that may be faced by liquidity management under different stress scenarios, and modified the liquidity emergency plan so as to ensure that risks can be effectively handled and controlled when the liquidity emergency plan is triggered.

##### (v) IT risks and their countermeasures

Information technology (IT) risk refers to the operational, legal and reputation risks generated due to natural factors, human factors, technical loopholes and management defects in the application process of information technology in banks. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened the IT risk management from the following aspects. Firstly, the Company intensified the governance of information technology by organizing and holding three meetings of the IT Management Committee, revising and improving 29 systems such as the Management Measures for Emergency Events of Information System, and improving the process and framework for managing IT risks. Secondly, the Company carried out the investigation and screening of IT risks, by comprehensively inspecting and screening out IT-related risks and hidden dangers through printing and issuing information security risk tips and carrying out information-assisted investigation of the defects of basic software and hardware products, and conducting systematic rectification and implementation, so as to further improve the management and control ability for IT risks. Thirdly, the Company strengthened the network risk monitoring by deploying the Internet behavior audit system of the Head Office and the Internet certification audit system of the Branches to realize the WeChat certification and audit based on the Internet; organizing special self-inspection and risk assessment for the wireless network security management, and carrying out the penetration test of the wireless network, so as to improve the Company's physical network security. Fourthly, the Company strengthened the continuity management of IT businesses, by organizing and carrying out more than 40 times of various emergency drills, such as the overall switching exercise of the power supply in the computer room of the Data Center, and the emergency drill of the citywide clearing system, so as to further improve the continuity of various businesses. Fifthly, the Company strengthened the protection of customer information, by setting up the work log for customer information protection, sorting out sensitive information of the production system, completing the shielding work of sensitive fields in batch exported reports and forms, and building the enterprise network disk, so as to strengthen the customer information protection.

## VIII. Completeness, rationality and effectiveness of the internal control system

The Company has been attaching great importance to the construction of the internal control system, adhering to the principles of comprehensiveness, prudence and effectiveness, and constantly improving the internal control management system, to ensure that all kinds of internal control systems and processes are scientific and rigorous, and also the Company's high-quality steady development. During the reporting period, the Company formulated the work plan for deepening the institutional construction, organized and carried out the publicity, training and competition of the systems throughout the Bank, and enhanced the awe awareness and execution ability of the whole staff on the systems. Meanwhile, according to the national policies and own development needs, the Company constantly improved and perfected the internal control system, and mapped the compliance management requirements and rules and regulations to various business activities, business lines, and employees on different posts. During the reporting

period, the Company's System Review Committee organized and held 11 review meetings to review 38 new systems.

By carrying out the systemic research, testing, analysis and assessment on the construction, implementation and operation results of the internal control system, the Company realized that the internal system basically covers all products, channels, transactions, management, and key links of the Bank, the process setting is scientific and reasonable, and effective implementation was done in standardizing the financial accounting behavior, improving the quality of accounting information, strengthening risk management, controlling operational risks, filling the gaps, and preventing frauds.

## （五）公司薪酬架构情况

### 1. 薪酬管理架构及决策程序

公司建立了科学的薪酬管理架构，明确规定董事、监事的报酬由股东大会决定，董事会决定高级管理人员的报酬。董事会对本行薪酬管理负最终责任，负责审阅、批准、修订本行薪酬管理制度与政策、薪酬成本与预算等，监督本行薪酬制度实施情况，并定期评估本行薪酬机制的健全性和有效性。董事会下设提名与薪酬考核委员会，负责审议有关薪酬制度、政策及监督工作。高级管理层负责实施董事会薪酬管理方面的决议，组织制定并更新本行薪酬管理制度与政策，管理和控制薪酬预算与成本，审阅、批准员工的定薪、调薪方案、绩效考核方案、员工福利方案等。

### 2. 年度薪酬总额与结构分布情况

2018年，公司薪酬总额为53181万元，薪酬结构分布如下：固定薪酬18460万元，占34.71%，可变薪酬19127万元，占35.97%，五险一金等福利性收入15594万元，占29.32%。

### 3. 薪酬与业绩衡量、风险调整的标准

公司总行部室员工绩效工资按照《莱商银行总行部室绩效考核管理办法》规定进行考核后兑现，分支行绩效工资按照《莱商银行绩效考核管理办法》进行考核后兑现。

### 4. 薪酬制度情况

以市场最佳实践为指引，确立了“市场导向、岗位导向、绩效导向、能力导向”的付薪理念，于2016年开展了薪酬体系优化设计项目，根据员工岗位职责及标准进行人岗匹配，实行了以岗定薪、按月拨付的固定薪酬制度，将薪酬总额与全行经营利润挂钩，建立自上而下的薪酬总额挂钩经营利润的提取机制，以及自下而上的薪酬总额核算机制，逐步健全全行薪酬总额管控机制。

明确了管理人员绩效薪酬延期支付相关规定，根据管理人员不同层级及岗位差异，将其绩效薪酬与工作责任和风险防控挂钩，并按照一定比例延期支付。

### 5. 年度薪酬方案制定及考核完成情况

公司年度薪酬方案根据人员规模、业务发展情况等制定，2018度，公司各项经营指标良好，薪酬情况合理的反映了公司业绩及风险管理情况。

6. 超出原定薪酬方案的例外情况

报告期内，公司无超出原定薪酬的例外情况。

## 二、员工情况

报告期末，公司在职员工人数为2144人；员工中本科及以上学历1916人，占比89.37%。

#### (v) Remunerations structure of the Company

## 1. Remunerations management structure and decision-making procedure

The company has established a scientific remuneration management structure, clearly stipulating that the remuneration of directors and supervisors shall be decided by the general meeting of shareholders, and the Board of Directors shall decide the remuneration of senior managers; the Board of Directors is ultimately responsible for the salary management of the Bank, responsible for reviewing, approving, revising the compensation management system and policies, and remuneration cost and budget of the Bank, supervising the implementation of the Bank's remuneration system, and regularly assessing the soundness and effectiveness of the remuneration mechanism; under the Board of Directors is the Nomination and Remuneration Assessment Committee, which is responsible for reviewing work relating to the remuneration system, policy and supervision; senior management is responsible for the implementation of resolutions of the Board of Directors about the remuneration management, organizing to develop and update the remuneration management system and policies, managing and controlling salary budget and cost, reviewing and approving the employees' wage fixing and salary increase plan, performance appraisal plan and staff benefit plan, etc.

## 2. Total Annual Compensation and Structure Distribution

In 2018, the company's total compensation was RMB531.81 million. The structure distribution of the compensation is as below: RMB184.60 million of fixed compensation, accounting for 34.71% of the total amount; RMB191.27 million of variable compensation, accounting for 35.97% of the total amount; RMB155.94 million of welfare income such as Five Insurances & Two Funds etc., accounting for 29.32% of the total amount.

3. The standard of remuneration and performance measurement and risk adjustment

Performance salary of the department / office employees of the Company’s head office shall be paid based on appraisal according to the provisions of the Management Measures for Performance Appraisal of Department / Office of Laishang Bank’s Head Office, and that of branch employees based on the provisions of the Management Measures for Performance Appraisal of Laishang Bank.

## 4. Remuneration system

Guided by the best practices of market, the Company established the salary payment concept of "market-oriented, position-oriented, performance-oriented, and ability-oriented". In 2016 the Company carried out the design project of optimizing the remuneration system, by person-post matching based on job responsibilities and standards of employees, implementing the fixed remuneration system, i.e., determining salary based on post and paying salary on a monthly basis, linking the total remuneration to the operating profit of the whole Bank, establishing the top-down extraction mechanism of linking the total remuneration to operating profit and the down-top calculation mechanism of the total remuneration, so as to gradually improve the management and control mechanism of the total compensation in the whole Bank.

The Company clearly defined the related provisions for deferred payment of management personnel's performance salary, so as to link their performance salary to job responsibilities, and risk prevention and control according to the different levels and positions of management personnel, and pay a certain proportion of performance salary in a deferred manner.

5. Preparation of annual remuneration plan and completion of assessment

The Company’s annual remuneration plan was prepared according to the size of staff, business development, and etc. In 2018, various operational indicators of the Company were good, and the remuneration reasonably reflected the performance and risk management of the Company.

6. Exceptions beyond the original remuneration plan

There was no exception beyond the original remuneration plan in the Company in the reporting period.

### II. Employee profile

At the end of the reporting period, there were 2,144 employees in the Company, among whom employees with a bachelor's degree or above amounted to 1,916, accounting for 89.37%.

## 第五章 公司治理结构

#### 一、公司治理情况

报告期内，公司严格遵守《公司法》和《商业银行法》的有关规定，认真落实监管部门关于公司治理的有关规定，致力于进一步完善公司治理结构，增强公司治理机制的有效性，提高董事会决策的科学性。公司治理具体情况如下：

（一）关于股东与股东大会。公司按照《公司章程》和《股东大会议事规则》的要求召集、召开股东大会，积极听取股东的意见和建议，确保股东对公司重大事项的知情权、参与权和表决权。报告期内，公司召开了2017年度股东大会、2018年第一次临时股东大会，会议严格按照有关规定履行了相关法律程序，保证了股东参会并行使权利，并由律师出具了法律意见书。

（二）关于董事和董事会。公司现有董事11名，其中独立董事3名。董事会能够严格按照《公司章程》的规定，认真履行职责，在股东大会授权下进行决策，强化公司治理，推进战略步伐，促进稳健经营，维护了公司和全体股东的利益。报告期内公司共召开了8次董事会会议。

目前，公司董事会下设1个办公室和5个专门委员会，包括董事会办公室、关联交易控制委员会、风险管理委员会、战略发展委员会、审计委员会、提名与薪酬考核委员会，其中风险管理委员会下设声誉风险管理专业委员会和信息科技风险管理专业委员会。5个专门委员会具有明确的职权范围，监管公司不同方面的事务，并对董事会负责，为董事会决策提供了建设性建议，进一步提高了董事会决策的科学性。

（三）关于监事和监事会。公司现有监事9人，其中职工监事3人，外部监事3人。监事会按照《公司章程》的规定认真履行职责，对公司财务、风险管理、资产质量以及部分高级管理人员履职的合规性进行监督检查，定期及不定期地与外部审计机构和管理层进行情况交流，有针对性地开展专项检查，针对风险隐患和发现的问题，向董事会和管理层提出建议和改进意见，从而更加有效地发挥监事会的作用。

（四）关于信息披露与透明度。公司在《金融时报》上发布了2017年度报告，并制作了《莱商银行股份有限公司2017年度报告》（摘要）单行本，及时准确地进行信息披露，切实维护广大股东特别是中小投资者的利益。

#### 二、独立董事及履行职责情况

报告期内，公司董事会现有独立董事3人，独立董事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加董事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）独立董事参加会议情况。报告期内，独立董事应参加董事会会议15人次，亲自出席15人次，委托出席0人次，缺席0人次。

（二）独立董事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司独立董事未对公司本年度董事会的议案提出异议。

#### 三、外部监事及履行职责情况

报告期内，公司监事会现有外部监事3人，外部监事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加监事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）外部监事参加会议情况。报告期内，公司监事应参加监事会会议8人次，亲自出席8人次，委托出席0人次，缺席0人次。

（二）外部监事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司外部监事未对公司本年度董事会、监事会的议案提出异议。

#### 四、公司经营决策体系

公司最高权力机构是股东大会，通过董事会、监事会进行管理和监督。行长受聘于董事会，对公司日常经营管理全面负责。公司实行一级法人体制，各分支机构均为非独立核算单位，其经营管理活动根据公司授权进行，并对公司负责。

公司无控股股东。公司与大股东在业务、人员、资产、机构、财务五方面完全独立。公司具有独立完整的业务及自主经营能力，董事会、监事会和内部机构能够独立运作。

#### 五、高级管理人员考评及激励机制、制度情况

报告期内，公司董事会进一步完善了对高级管理人员的绩效考核管理办法，建立了薪酬与公司效益和个人业绩相联系的激励约束机制；考核实行百分制，根据各高管人员的工作性质不同，考核项目有所侧重。对于非执行董事、非职工监事的考核主要以其出席会议次数及履职能力考核为主。制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会董事履职评价制度》，主要分为客观评价、自我评价和相互评价三个部分，根据各项指标具体内容的不同，给予不同的比重。公司制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会对高管人员履职评价办法》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事、监事、高管人员履职评价办法》、《关于追究董事、监事及高管人员责任的规定》。同时，公司监事会进一步量化考核标准，制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高管人员履职评价办法实施细则》，明确了监事会对高级管理人员的履职评价，加大了履职问责力度。

## Chapter V Corporate Governance Structure

### 1. Corporate governance of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company has strictly observed the related provisions in the Company Law and the Law on Commercial Banks, conscientiously implemented the related regulations of the regulatory intuition on corporate governance, and been dedicated to further improving corporate governance structure, enhancing the effectiveness of the corporate governance mechanism and making the decision of the Board of Directors more scientific. The performance of the Company in its corporate governance is as follows:

##### (i) Shareholders and shareholders' general meeting.

The Company has convened the shareholders’ general meeting in accordance with the requirement of the Articles of Association and the Rules of Procedures for Shareholders’ Meeting and actively listened to shareholders’ opinion and suggestion to ensure that all shareholders have the right to know, to participate and to vote to the major events of the Company. During the reporting period, the Company held its 2017 annual shareholders’ general meeting, and 2018 first interim shareholders’ general meeting. The meetings went through related legal procedures in strict accordance with the related regulations to ensure that shareholders participate in the meetings and exercise their right. Also, legal opinion was issued by a lawyer.

##### (ii) Directors and the Board of Directors.

Currently, the Company has 11 directors, including 3 independent directors. The Board of Directors is able to conscientiously perform its duties in strict accordance with the provisions in the Articles of Association, make decisions with the authorization of the shareholders' general meeting, strengthen the corporate governance, advance strategic steps and promote steady operation which safeguarded the interests of the Company and all shareholders. During the reporting period, the Company held 8 board meetings totally.

Currently, under the Board of Directors of the Company there is 1 office and 5 special committees, including the Office of Board of Directors, Related Transaction Control Committee, Risk Management Committee, Strategic Development Committee, Audit Committee, and Nomination and Remuneration Assessment Committee. Under Risk Management Committee there are the Special Committee of Reputation Risk Management and Special Committee of IT Risk Management. These 5 special committees have specific scopes of power, supervise and manage the matters at different aspects, and are accountable to the Board of Directors. They provide constructive suggestions for the decision making of the Board of Directors in a timely manner to improve the scientific of the decisions made by the Board of Directors.

#### (iii) Supervisors and the Board of Supervisors.

The Company has 9 supervisors, including 3 employee supervisors and 3 external supervisors.

The Board of Supervisors conscientiously performs its duties in strict accordance with the provisions in the Articles of Association, carries out supervision and inspection over the compliance of the Company's finance, risk management, asset quality and the duty performance of part of the senior management, communicates with the external audit units and management regularly and irregularly, conducts targeted special inspections, and puts forward suggestions and improvement opinions to the Board of Directors and the senior management according to hidden risks or problems discovered, so as to play the role of the Board of Supervisors more effectively.

##### (iv) Information disclosure and transparency.

The Company has published its 2017 Annual Report on Financial Times, and prepared a separate edition of 2017 Annual Report of Laishang Co., Ltd. (Summary), to carry out information disclosure in a timely and accurate manner and safeguard the interests of shareholders, particularly medium and small shareholders.

## II. Independent directors and their performance of duties

During the reporting period, there were 3 independent directors on the Board of Directors. With an attitude of being responsible to all shareholders, these independent directors performed the obligation of integrity and diligence, earnestly participated in the board meetings and meetings of various special committees, and actively delivered their opinions to safeguard the collective interest of the Company and the legitimate rights and interest of medium and small shareholders.

##### (i) Independent directors' participation in meetings.

During the reporting period, the number of independent directors attending the board meetings should be 15 person-times, with presence in 15 person-times, entrusted attendance in 0 person-times, and absence in 0 person-times.

##### (ii) Objections of the independent directors to related affairs of the Company.

During the reporting period, the independent directors of the Company did not object to any proposal of the Board of Directors of the current year.

## III. External supervisors and their performance of duties

There were 3 external supervisors in the Board of Supervisors in the Company during the report period. With an attitude of being responsible to all shareholders, these external supervisors performed their duty of integrity and diligence, carefully participated in the meeting of the Board of Supervisors and all special committee meetings, positively gave opinions and prevent overall benefit of the Company and lawful rights and interests of medium and small shareholders from infringement.

##### (i) Presence of external supervisors in meetings.

During the reporting period, the number of the external supervisors attending the Board of Supervisors Meeting should be 8 person-times, with presence in 8 person-times, entrusted attendance

in 0 person-times and absence in 0 person-times.

##### (ii) Objection raised by the external supervisors.

During the reporting period, the external supervisors did not raise objections to the proposals of the Board of Directors and Board of Supervisors of the Company of the current year.

## IV. Operation and decision-making system of the Company

The highest authority of the Company is the Shareholders' General Meeting, which is managed and supervised through the Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors. The president, employed by the Board of Directors, is responsible for all daily operation and management of the Company. Only the head office of the Company is its legal person, and all branches and sub-branches are non-independent accounting units, whose operation and management activities shall be carried out with the authorization of the Company and who shall be responsible for the Company.

The Company has no controlling shareholder. The Company and its majority shareholders are completely independent in terms of business, personnel, assets, organization and finance. The Company has independent and complete business and independent operation ability, and the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and internal organizations can operate independently.

## V.Assessment and incentive mechanism and system of senior management

During the reporting period, the Board of Directors of the Company further improved the method of performance assessment and management of its senior management, and established an incentive and constraint mechanism in which remunerations are associated with the performance of the Company and personal performance; the centesimal system has been implemented for assessment subject to the working nature of senior management, and different management positions have different key points for assessment. The assessment of non-executive directors and non-employee supervisors has mainly focused on their attendance on meetings and ability to perform their duties. The Evaluation System of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Duty Performance of Directors of the Board of Directors, which includes three parts, i.e., objective evaluation, self-evaluation and mutual evaluation, has been prepared. Different proportions are given according to different contents of various indexes. The Measures of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Senior Management's Performance, the Measures of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management's Performance and the Provisions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Accountability of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management have been formulated. Meanwhile, the Board of Supervisors further quantified the criteria of assessment, formulated the Implementing Rules of Duty Performance Assessment Method on Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., specified the duty performance assessment on senior management by the Board of Supervisors and strengthened the accountability of duty performance.

## 第六章 股东大会情况

报告期内公司共召开了两次股东大会，具体情况如下：

2018年4月25日，在公司综合营业楼一楼礼堂召开了2017年度股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共48名，代表股份273182.8322万股，占公司总股本的91.06%。大会审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会2017年度工作报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会2017年度工作报告》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司2017年度审计报告>的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2017年度财务报告及利润分配方案》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司2018年度预算>的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2017年度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2017年度风险管理报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2017年度消费者权益保护工作报告》《关于对〈莱商银行股份有限公司2017年度报告〉进行信息披露的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事2017年度履职评价报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高级管理人员2017年度履职评价报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对监事2017年度履职评价报告》《关于对莱芜钢铁集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《关于对齐鲁交通发展集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》。

2018年12月26日，在公司综合营业楼六楼会议室召开了2018年第一次临时股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共47名，代表股份269052.7852万股，占公司总股本的89.68%。大会审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司股权管理办法》《关于修改<莱商银行股份有限公司章程>的议案》《关于选举兰翠云为第四届监事会外部监事的议案》。

## Chapter VI Shareholders' General Meeting

During the reporting period, the Company held two general meetings of shareholders, with the details as follows.

On April 25, 2018, the 2017 Annual Shareholders' General Meeting was held in the auditorium on the first floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 48 shareholders and authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 2,731,828,322 shares, accounting for 91.06% of the Company's total share capital. The meeting deliberated and passed the 2017 Annual Work Report of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Work Report of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on 2017 Annual Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Financial Report and Profit Distribution Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on 2018 Annual Budget of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Report on Executive Conditions of Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Report on Consumer Protection of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Information Disclosure for '2017 Annual Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.' 2017 Annual Evaluation Report on Duty Performance of Directors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Evaluation Report on Duty Performance of Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Evaluation Report on Duty Performance of Supervisors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Granting for Laiwu Iron&Steel Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises, Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Granting for Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises.

On December 26, 2018, the First Interim Shareholders' General Meeting of 2018 was held in the meeting room on the sixth floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 47 shareholders and authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 2,690,527,852 shares, accounting for 89.68% of the Company's total share capital. The meeting deliberated and passed the Management Measures for Equity of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Revising the Articles of Association of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Electing Lan Cuiyun as External Supervisor of the Fourth Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

## 第七章 董事会报告

## 一、2018年度主要经营管理情况

## （一）聚焦转型深化改革，各项业务实现了稳健、持续发展

截至2018年12月31日，公司资产总额1213.20亿元，各项存款余额769.62亿元，各项贷款余额629.82亿元，公司实现拨备前利润（税前）15.65亿元，提取拨备9.6亿元，利润总额6.05亿元，缴纳税金4.25亿元，每股净资产3.01元，发展质量和效益稳步提升，在英国银行家杂志评选的2018年全球银行1000强中，莱商银行名列第599位。

## （二）充分发挥战略决策职能，全力推动转型发展

一是保持战略定力，加快转型发展。2018年，董事会着眼长远、立足当前，积极适应金融发展新形势，高度重视金融科技应用，坚定支持高级管理层深入推进经营转型，将资产负债结构调整作为提升企业效益的总抓手，加大资产流转速度，优化业务结构，着力提高风险资产收益率。2018年，全行收入结构进一步优化，营收稳定性进一步增强，在保持对公业务优势的同时，零售业务的价值贡献度持续提升。二是积极推进对标达标与精细化管理。扎实推进基础管理水平提升，进一步理顺条线管理体制，细化工作要求和标准，按照“四环节工作法”的要求，落实具体责任人和工作步骤，明确界定各项工作责任，制定切实工作计划，扎实开展对标达标与精细化管理工作。通过与选定的标杆进行全面对标，研究与标杆单位在管理方式、业务流程和经营指标方面的差距，对比分析差距原因，结合自身实际，制定得力措施，提升全行基础管理水平。三是进一步理顺管理机制，提高服务支撑能力。稳步推进行分组织架构调整，明晰总分行条线管理架构。坚持原则性与灵活性相结合，对分行部室进行合理设置，管理条线更加清晰，部室及岗位职责边界更加明确，向一线、基层倾斜的用人导向更加凸显。四是持续加强对外投资的管理。协助河南方城凤裕村镇银行、天津武清村镇银行拟定更换董事、董事长的相关议案和流程；对四家村镇银行2017年度董事会工作报告、经营管理报告、利润分配方案等议案进行了重点审议，并对四家村镇银行进行调研形成报告；针对东营莱商村镇银行、五台莱商村镇银行经营管理、资产质量、风险管控等相关情况进行了实地调研，并对村镇银行下一步发展提出了意见和建议。

## （三）强化全面风险管理和内部控制，基础管理能力稳步提升

一是深入开展资产质量攻坚。不断拓宽不良资产处置渠道，积极争取地方政府及大型优质企业的支持，通过担保代偿、以物抵债、法律诉讼、风险代理等多种方式全力化解不良资产。二是全面落实不良贷款责任认定工作。严格遵循“以事实为依据，抓住关键节点、重要环节，区别关键少数，责权匹配，尽职免责”原则，细化了“责任认定、新老划断、重在当下”三原则的标准和方法，坚持还原场景、细分责任、精准问责，认定过程中对未形成新的不良或未扩大损失的尽

职行为予以免责，积极、有效地引导全行不良贷款处置工作。三是完善全面风险管理体系。完善风险管理政策体系，制定《莱商银行全面风险管理指导意见》《莱商银行风险偏好管理办法》等制度，进一步明确全面风险管理部门、风险管理主控部门及责任部门的职责。上线运行操作风险管理系统，涵盖合规管理、操作风险管理、内部控制评价等7个模块23项功能，制定《莱商银行2018年深化制度建设工作方案》，组织开展制度宣讲13次，有效调动全员学习制度、执行制度的积极性、主动性。四是全面推进深化整治市场乱象工作。制定《莱商银行进一步深化整治银行业市场乱象工作实施方案》，编印《银监会专项治理文件汇编》，完成整治乱象自查评估及阶段性“上查下”工作，完成整治乱象年度工作报告。针对检查发现问题，完善制度和管理流程，督导分支机构扎实开展问责与整改工作，切实做到系统性整改。

## （四）健全资本管理和约束机制，夯实经营发展的资本基础

一是强化资本节约理念。2017年末完成增资扩股后，全行的资本水平虽然得到了有效提升，但由于资本的稀缺性，资本管理的重要性凸显，为此强化了对风险资本回报率的考核，加大了对风险加权资产占用率的考核比重，引导分支机构加快资产结构调整，积极发展轻资本业务，降低风险资本消耗速度。二是积极组织开展内部资本充足评估和压力测试。组织开展了年度内部资本充足评估和压力测试工作，全面评估本行面临的各类实质性风险，及其对本行资本充足状况的影响，为全行内部资本补充提供决策依据，为全行资本使用节约提供了规范标准。三是积极探索新的资本补充渠道。根据《关于进一步支持商业银行资本工具创新的意见》，对目前无固定期限资本债券、转股型二级资本债券等新型资本工具进行了调研，结合全行对未来三年资本缺口的测算，初步拟定了未来资本补充的路径，拟于2019年启动资本补充，为全行各项业务发展提供资本支撑。

## （五）积极履行社会责任，着力提升品牌价值

一是全力做好对外宣传。加强与新闻媒体的交流合作，加大正面宣传报道力度，年内向莱芜银行业协会、银监局、市政府信息科、市人行、金融办等报送信息160余条次，在《莱芜日报》金融周刊中发布信息40余篇，正向引导社会舆论，提升了莱商银行社会形象。同时，在莱芜电视台《藏在金融》栏目，进行全行形象和业务产品宣传，取得了较好效果。二是深入开展消费者权益保护工作。2018年，全行以监管部门的消费者权益保护工作考核评价办法为基准，继续做好消费者权益保护各项工作，把消保工作深化到与消费者相关的各领域、各条线、各业务范畴，深化到各分支机构。持续做好消费者权益保护制度体系完善工作，优化产品与服务管理机制，通过制度的修订完善明确消费者权益保护内容，进一步丰富产品与服务风险披露的内容和形式，落实产品销售透明性。三是积极参与各类慈善公益事业。积极参与“春蕾计划”“希望工程”、慈善募捐等活动，竭力倡导奉献精神和社会责任意识，2018年莱芜地区捐助春蕾儿童191人，捐款金额6万余元。与市总工会、区总工会联合，在莱芜辖内14家网点建立爱心驿站，为环卫工人、农民工等社会群体提供了休息场所。各级工会扎根当地、服务当地，利用端午、中秋、重阳等传统节

日，组织员工到敬老院、儿童福利院、孤寡老人、困难群众家中走访慰问，用实际行动帮扶困难群众。四是持续加强人本管理。完善现行人事制度体系，印发《莱商银行员工管理办法》《莱商银行员工岗位交接管理办法》《莱商银行员工及时奖励办法》，提高人事管理水平。强化教育培训，制定年度培训计划，努力提高培训的针对性与实效性，提高员工及管理人员的综合素养。借助外部权威师资力量，举办两期中高层管理人员脱产培训、两期内训师培训、一期后备干部培训，有效提高了中高层管理人员、后备干部的综合素养及内训师工作质量。大力推进暖心工程，建立职工互助基金，坚持把温暖送到员工心中生活中，有效减轻困难职工负担。

## 四、2019年董事会工作计划

## (一) 工作思路：

以党的十九大精神和习近平新时代中国特色社会主义思想为指导，按照“一个中心、两个坚持、三个提升、四个加强”的战略部署，高举“高质量稳健发展”的大旗，坚持“稳中求进”的总基调，继续实施“党建统领、改革提质、创新提效、管理提速”四轮驱动，突出“提高发展质量”和“防范化解风险”两大主题，紧扣合规经营的总要求，努力做到“四个全面”：即全面提升基础管理水平，全面加大对新旧动能转换的支持力度，全面推进资产质量攻坚，全面提高团队综合素质，全力以赴地保持高质量稳健发展的良好势头。

## (二) 工作目标:

1. 确保资本充足率达到监管要求；

2. 存款总额达到835亿元，同比增长79亿元，增幅超过10%，存款偏离度符合监管要求；

3. 贷款总额达到690亿元，同比增长68亿元，增幅超过10%；

4. 实现拨备前利润（税前）16.72亿元；

5. 不良贷款率控制在3%以内，拨备覆盖率在150%以上，拨贷比达到2.5%以上；

6. 实现全年安全营运，无道德案件和重大责任案件发生。

## (三) 工作重点:

1. 持续完善中国特色公司治理，提升董事会和董事的履职能力。健全完善“党委领导核心、董事会战略决策、监事会依法监督、高管层全权经营”的治理模式，实现各治理主体权责明确、有效制衡和协调运作，推动服务实体经济、防控金融风险、深化金融改革。加强董事会对战略管理、全面风险管理、激励约束、资本管理、内部控制等重点治理领域的最终责任，强化战略执行和成效评估。持续加强董事履职能力，通过听取汇报、开展调研、参加培训等方式，确保董事能够深入了解全行经营管理情况及监管政策、行业热点，持续提升履职能力。

2. 着力推动转型发展。强化顶层设计，围绕“五年再造”的战略目标，站在更高的起点谋划和推进改革路径，持续推动中长期发展规划的落地执行。强化战略执行力评估，对战略规划的

落地情况和执行效果进行监督和评估，了解战略执行过程中遇到的问题，分析战略实施层面遇到的困难，推动服务全行战略的有效实施。抓住济莱两市合并带来的发展机遇，利用济南区域金融中心的优势，持续挖掘重大战略性业务机会、改革转型和创新发展机遇，助力莱商银行实现新发展。

3. 提升全面风险管理体系的有效性。继续坚持审慎稳健的风险偏好，根据经济金融环境及全行实际，合理设定年度风险容忍度和风险限额，形成清晰明确的风险偏好治理架构。完善全面风险管理体系，加快风险监测体系建设，做好流动性与市场风险管理，满足宏观审慎评估体系（MPA）、流动性覆盖率（LCR）等监管要求。建立资产质量管控长效机制，加强贷款分类管理、拨备管理和核销管理，确保各项资产质量指标持续向好。

4. 持续提升资本管理能力。继续根据业务发展需要，积极运用合适的资本工具，拓宽资本补充渠道，优化资本结构，在加强资本动态管理的同时，提升资本使用效率，保障公司质量、效益、规模均衡发展。密切关注无固定期限资本债券的政策动态，适时启动一级资本补充，同时合理规划资本使用，约束资本消耗，引导经营机构挖掘内部潜力，寻求资本节约空间，实现资本和收益平衡。

5. 积极推动消费者权益保护工作。制定全行消费者权益保护工作的战略、政策和目标，不断健全消费者权益保护工作的制度体系，切实将消费者权益保护融入公司治理、经营目标、企业文化建设中，并做好相关工作的信息披露，持续跟踪监督、评价消费者权益保护工作的全面性、及时性、有效性，定期听取年度消费者保护工作专项报告，探索消费者权益保护工作考核评价体系，并合理运用考核结果。

6. 不断加强团队建设。加强人才引进工作管理，通过多种渠道，引进部分经验丰富的管理人员及专业技术人员，建立更具约束性和激励性的薪酬管理体系，为全行业务发展提供人才保障。统筹规划、分层次开展员工教育培训，强化人才梯队建设，并着力提高内部培训的针对性及实用性；开展中高层管理人员培训，提高培训组织、考评的精细化水平，提升培训实效。加强后备干部及内训师队伍管理，建立、完善能进能出的动态考核管理机制，提升内训师队伍整体素养。

7. 着力提升品牌形象。加强硬广与软媒的有机结合，加强对新媒体宣传的运用，拓宽宣传渠道。主动加强与大众舆情信息监测平台的合作交流，保证正面信息宣传和防控负面舆情两手抓、两手硬，加大声誉风险防范力度。积极践行社会责任，持续推进绿色信贷、普惠金融等政策措施，积极推进新旧动能转换，支持实体经济发展。继续参与公益事业，通过整合内外部资源，系统性、持续性地开展公益活动，积极回报社会。

## 五、利润分配方案

2018年度计提盈余公积金46,310,359.17元，计提任意盈余公积金9,262,071.83元，提取一般风险准备304,485,251.10元；暂不进行现金分红；剩余未分配利润106,747,427.01元结转以后年度。

### Chapter VII Report of the Board of Directors

### I. Review of main operation and management in 2018

## (i) The Company focused on transformation and deepened the reform, and realized the steady and sustainable development in terms of all businesses.

As of December 31, 2018, the Company's total assets reached RMB 121.320 billion; the balance of various deposits was RMB 76.962 billion; the balance of various loans was RMB 62.982 billion; the profit before provision (pre-tax) was RMB 1,565 million; the amount for extraction and provision was RMB 960 million; the total profit was RMB 605 million; the taxes paid reached RMB 425 million; the net asset value per share was RMB 3.01, development quality and benefits have been improved steadily and Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. was ranked the 599th among the Top 1,000 Global Banks, appraised and selected by magazine British Banker in 2018.

## (ii) The Company gave full play to the strategic decision-making functions and made every effort to promote the transformation and development.

Firstly, the Company maintained the strategic focus and accelerated the transformation and development. In 2018, the Board of Directors took a long-term perspective, based on the current situations, actively adapted to the new conditions of financial development, attached great importance to the application of financial technology, firmly supported the senior management to further advance the operating transformation, deemed the structure adjustment of assets and liabilities as the overall starting point to improve the enterprise benefit, increased the turnover rate of assets, optimized the business structure, and tried to increase the return rates of risky assets. In 2018, the income structure of the whole Bank was further optimized and the revenue stability further enhanced. While the advantages of the business to corporates were maintained, the value contribution degree of the retail business continued to be increased. Secondly, the Company actively promoted to setting-and-attaining targets and refined management, by steadily promoting the basic management level, further rationalizing the horizontal and longitudinal management systems, detailing the work requirements and standards; implementing the specific responsible persons and work steps, and clearly defining various work responsibilities in accordance with the requirements of the "four-link work method", so as to steadily setting-and-attaining targets and also carry out refined management; by comprehensively benchmarking against the selected targets, researching the gap in management methods, business processes and business indicators with the targets units, comparing and analyzing the reasons for the gaps, and formulating effective measures in combination with the own reality, so as to improve the basic management level of the whole Bank. Thirdly, the Company further rationalized the management mechanism and improved the service support capacity. Steadily promoting the organizational structure adjustment of the Branches, clarifying the business line management structure of the Branches. Adhering to the combination of principle and flexibility, reasonably setting the departments in the Branches, making the management lines and also the

responsibility boundaries of departments and posts clearer, and highlighting more the employment orientation of declining to the frontline and grass-roots posts; starting the organizational structure adjustment of the Laiwu Head Office to further improve the management mechanism and streamline the departments and staffing. As a result, the original 13 departments were reduced to 9 according to the principles of "matching personnel and post and making incentive compatible", which strengthened the business line management, realized the separation of the front, middle and back stages, effectively adjusted the structure of the management personnel, and improved the overall appearance of the team. Fourthly, the Company continued to strengthen the management of external investment by assisting Henan Fangcheng Fengyu Village Bank and Tianjin Wuqing Village Bank in drafting the relevant proposals and procedures for the replacement of the directors and presidents; deliberating the 2017 Annual Work Report of the Board of Directors, 2017 Annual Business Management Report, 2017 Annual Profit Distribution Plan and other proposals of the four village banks, and investigating them and forming the research reports; carrying out the on-site investigation and research on the operation and management, asset quality, risk management and control, and other related situations of Dongying Laishang Village Bank and Wutai Laishang Village Bank, and putting forward opinions and suggestions on the further development of these village banks.

## (iii) The Company strengthened the comprehensive risk management and internal control, and steadily improved the basic management ability.

Firstly, the Company deepened the efforts to improve the asset quality, by continuing to expand the channels for disposing non-performing assets, actively seeking for the support of local governments and large high-quality enterprises, and trying the best to resolve non-performing assets by means of guarantee for compensation, debt repayment in kind, legal proceedings and risk agency. Secondly, the Company comprehensively implemented the responsibility confirmation of non-performing loans, by strictly adhering to the principles of "basing on facts, seizing key nodes and important links, distinguishing key minorities, matching responsibility and authority, and fulfilling due diligence and exemption", detailing the criteria and methods for the three principles of "responsibility identification, separating old and new ones, and focusing on current ones", insisting on the recovering scenarios, subdividing responsibilities, and precision accountability, exempting liabilities from the due diligence behavior from which no new non-performing asset is formed or no loss enlarged in the confirmation process, and actively and effectively guiding the disposal work of non-performing loans of the whole bank. Thirdly, the Company improved the comprehensive risk management system, by perfecting the risk management policy system, formulating the Guidance on Comprehensive Risk Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Management Measures for Risk Preference of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and other systems, and further clarifying the duties and responsibilities of the comprehensive risk management departments, and main control departments and responsible departments for risk management; putting the operational risk management system into operation, covering 23 functions of 7 modules, including compliance management, operational risk management and internal control evaluation, formulating the 2018 Work Plan for Deepening Institutional Construction of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and organizing the system publicity for 13 times, so as to effectively mobilize the enthusiasm and initiative of all staff in learning and implementing the systems. Fourthly, the Company comprehensively promoted and deepened the

efforts to rectify and control market chaos, by formulating the Work Implementation Plan for Further Rectifying Market Chaos of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., compiling and printing the Assembly of the Special Governance Files Issued by the China Banking Regulatory Commission (CBRC), completing the self-inspection & evaluation and periodic work from top down on rectification of market chaos, the Annual Report on Rectification of Market Chaos was completed; improving the system and management process according to the problems found in the inspections, and supervising the Branches to carry out the accountability and rectification work, so as to achieve systematic rectification.

## (iv) The Company improved the capital management and restraint mechanism to consolidate the capital base for business development.

Firstly, the Company strengthened the concept of capital saving. After completing the increase in capital and share at the end of 2017, although the capital level of the whole Bank has been effectively promoted, the importance of capital management was highlighted due to the scarcity of capital. For this, the Company strengthened the evaluation on the return rates of risky capitals, by increasing the appraisal proportion of the occupancy rate of risk-weighted assets, guiding the Branches to speed up the asset structure adjustment, vigorously developing light capital-related business, and slowing down the consumption speed of risky capital. Secondly, the Company actively organized and carried out internal assessment on capital adequacy and stress tests, by organizing the annual internal assessment on capital adequacy, comprehensively evaluating various substantive risks faced by the Bank and their impact on the Bank's capital adequacy, so as to provide decision-making basis for the Bank's internal capital supplement and criteria and standards for the Bank's capital use and saving. Thirdly, the Company actively explored new capital supplement channels, by investigating and researching such new capital instruments as non-fixed capital bonds and secondary capital bonds convertible to stocks according to the "Opinions about Further Support of Innovating Capital Instruments of Commercial Banks", preliminarily deciding the routes for future capital supplement in combination with the measurement and calculation of the capital gaps of the whole Bank in the next three years, and planning to launch capital supplement in 2019, so as to provide capital support for all kinds of business development of the whole Bank.

## (v) The Company actively fulfilled the social responsibilities and strived to enhance brand value.

Firstly, the Company fully tried to promote the publicity to the outside world, by strengthening the exchange and cooperation with news media, increasing the intensity of positive publicity, and reporting more than 160 messages to the Laiwu Banking Association, Banking Supervisory Bureau, Information Department of the Laiwu Municipal Government, Laiwu Municipal People's Bank, Finance Office, and so on, releasing more than 40 messages in the Laiwu Daily, Financial Weekly, and the like, so as to positively guide public opinion and promote the social image of Laishang Bank, and conducting the publicity of the image and business products of the whole Bank in the column "Win in Finance" of Laiwu TV Station at the same time, from which good results were achieved. Secondly, the Company deeply protected the rights and interests of consumers in 2018, by continuing to do a good job in various aspects of the protection of consumers' rights and interests in the whole Bank based on the assessment and appraisal methods of the regulatory authorities for

the protection work of consumers' rights and interests, and deepening the consumer protection to all fields, business lines, and business areas related to consumers, as well as all branches; continuously improving the system for consumer protecting, optimizing the management mechanism of products and services, perfecting and clarifying the content of consumer protecting through the revision of the system, further enriching the contents and forms of the risk disclosure of products and services, and implementing the transparency of product sales. Thirdly, the Company actively participated in various programs of public welfare and charity, by doing so in the activities such as "Spring Bud Plan", "Hope Project", and donation for charity, to try to advocate the spirit of dedication and sense of social responsibility. In 2018, the Company donated 191 children in Laiwu included in the "Spring Bud Plan", with a donation amount of more than RMB 60,000, established love stations in 14 branches in Laiwu jointly with the Laiwu Municipal and District-level Federations of Trade Unions, providing places for social groups such as sanitation workers and migrant workers to rest in. The Trade Unions at all levels took root and served locally by organizing the employees to visit nursing homes of the elderly, children's welfare homes, families of elderly persons alone, and families of poor people, and send living supplies and sincere greetings to them during the Dragon Boat Festival, Mid-Autumn Festival, Double Ninth Festival and other traditional festivals, so as to help those poor people with practical actions. Fourthly, the Company continued to strengthen the people-oriented management, by improving the current personnel system, printing and issuing the Staff Management Measures of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Management Measures for Post Handover of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and Timely Reward Measures for Staff of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., so as to improve the level of personnel management; strengthened the education and training by formulating the Annual Training Plan, striving to improve the pertinence and effectiveness of training, improving the comprehensive quality of employees and managers, and holding two off-job training sessions for senior management personnel, two training sessions for internal trainers, and one training session for reserved cadres with the help of external authoritative teachers, so as to effectively improve the comprehensive quality of middle and senior management personnel and reserved cadres as well as the quality of internal trainers; vigorously promoted the Heart-Warming Project, by establishing the mutual fund for workers and adhering to delivering warmth to the life and hearts of employees, so as to effectively reduce the burden of difficult workers.

## III. Daily work of the Board of Directors

The Board of Directors of the Company held five general meetings of shareholders, with the details as follows during the reporting period.

(I) On March 29, 2018, the 15th Meeting of the 4th Board of Directors of the Company was held. The meeting deliberated and passed the 2017 Annual Work Report of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Operating Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Financial Report and Profit Distribution Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on 2018 Annual Budget of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on 2018 Annual IT Budget of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Report on IT Work

and Plan Execution of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Report on Internal Audit of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Evaluation Report on Internal Control of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Report on Consumer Protecting of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Report on Executive Conditions of Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Reporting the Registration Business of the Board of Directors of Q4 2017, Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Granting for Laiwu Steel Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises, Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Granting for Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises, Proposal on Pledge of Equity Held in Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. by Tai'an Financial Holding Group Co., Ltd., Proposal on Information Disclosure for '2017 Annual Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.' Evaluation Report on 2017 Annual Duty Performance of Directors by the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Evaluation Report on Evaluation Report on 2017 Annual Duty Performance of Senior Management by the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Comprehensive Risk Management Policies of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Further Standardizing Remuneration of Senior Executives of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and Proposal on Holding the 2017 Annual General Meeting of Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

(II) On June 25, 2018, the 16th Meeting of the 4th Board of Directors of the Company was held in a communication manner. The meeting deliberated and passed the Operating Work Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q1 2018, Proposal on Adjusting Composition of Members of All Special Committees of the Fourth Board of Directors, Management Measures for Risk Preference of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2018 Annual Strategy for Risk Preference of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Evaluation Report on Internal Capital Adequacy of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Report on Executive Conditions of Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q1 2018, Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q1 2018, and Proposal on Reporting Registration Business to the Board of Directors of Q1 2018.

(III) On August 14, 2018, the 17th Meeting of the 4th Board of Directors of the Company was held. The meeting deliberated and passed the Operating Work Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of January to June 2018, Proposal on the 'Equity Management Measures of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.' Proposal on Application of Director Zhao Huaifang for Resignation, Proposal on Dismissing the Deputy President Zhao Huaifang of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Appointing Liu Haixin as Chief Financial Officer of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Transferring Equity Held in Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. by Laiwu Municipal Bureau of Finance to Laiwu Municipal Economic Development & Investment Co., Ltd., Proposal on Pledge of Equity Held in Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. by Heze Urban Construction Engineering Development Group Co., Ltd., Report on Executive Conditions of Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q2 2018, Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q2 2018, Proposal on Reporting Registration Business to the Board of Directors of Q2 2018, Proposal on 2018 Annual Planning to Write-off Non-performing Loans, and IT Work Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of January to

June 2018.

(IV) On November 29, 2018, the 18th meeting of the 4th Board of Directors of the Company was held. The meeting deliberated and passed the Proposal on Transferring Equity Held in Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. by Panda Financial Holding Corp., Ltd. to Guangyang Antai Holding Co., Ltd., and Proposal on Centralized Disposal and Settlement of Some Non-Performing Assets.

(V) On December 10, 2018, the 19th meeting of the 4th Board of Directors of the Company was held in a communication manner. The meeting deliberated and passed the Proposal on Revising the Articles of Association of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Authorization of President of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. by the Board of Directors, Report on Executive Conditions of Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q3 2018, Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q3 2018, and Proposal on Holding the First Interim Shareholders' General Meeting of 2018

## IV. 2019 Work Plan of the Board of Directors

#### (i) Thinking of work

Guided by the spirit of the 19th National Congress of the Communist Party of China and Xi Jinping Thought on Socialism with Chinese Characteristics for a New Era, the Company's Board of Directors continued to follow the strategic deployment of "One Center, Two Persistence, Three Promotion, & Four Strengthening", highly hold the banner of high quality and steady development, insist on the overall tone of "making progress while ensuring stability", implement the policies of "Party building assurance, reform-driven quality, innovation-based efficiency and management efficiency", highlight the major themes of "improving development quality and preventing and solving risks", closely fulfill the general requirement of compliant operation, make efforts to realize "Four comprehensiveness" i.e., comprehensively enhance the level of basic management, comprehensively support the conversion between old and new kinetic energy, comprehensively promote the asset quality, and comprehensively improve the overall quality of the team and maintain a good momentum of sustainable, sound and rapid development.

#### (ii) Work objectives

1. Ensuring that capital adequacy ratio should meet the regulatory requirements;

2. Ensuring the total deposits hit RMB 83.5 billion, representing an increase of RMB 7.9 billion or 10%, and deposit deviation should meet the regulatory requirements;

3. The total loans are planned to hit RMB 69 billion, representing an increase of RMB 6.8 billion or 10% by the year end.

4. Realizing a profit of RMB 1.672 billion before provision (before taxes);

5. The non-performing loan rate always controlled with in 3%, the loan loss provision adequacy rate above 150%, and the provision/loan reaching above 2.5%;

6. Realizing safe operation and absence of moral cases and major responsibility cases in the

whole year.

##### (iii) Work focus

1. To continuously improve the corporate governance with Chinese characteristics and enhance the ability of the Board of Directors and directors to perform their duties, by establishing and improving the governance mode featuring "core leadership from the Party Committee, strategic decision-making from the Board of Directors, supervision from the Board of Supervisors by law, and full management from senior management", ensuring that all governance bodies have clear powers and responsibilities, effectively balancing and coordinating their operations, promoting service for the real economy, preventing and controlling financial risks, and deepening financial reform; strengthening the ultimate responsibility of the Board of Directors for strategic management, comprehensive risk management, incentive and restraint, capital management, internal control and other key governance areas, and enhancing strategy implementation and effectiveness evaluation; continuing to strengthen the ability of directors to perform their duties, and ensuring that directors can have an in-depth understanding of the operation and management situations of the whole Bank, regulatory policies, and hot spots in the industry, and continuously improve their ability to perform their duties by listening to reports, conducting investigation and research, attending training and other means.

2. To try to promote transformation and development, by strengthening top-level design, centering on the strategic goal of "five-year reconstruction", planning and promoting reform from a higher starting point, and continuously promoting the implementation of the medium-and-long-term development plans; strengthening the evaluation on strategic execution, supervising and evaluating the implementation situation and effect of strategic planning, understanding the problems encountered in the implementation process of strategies, analyzing the difficulties encountered in the implementation of strategies, and promoting the effective implementation of strategies of the whole Bank; seizing the development opportunities brought by the merger of Jinan and Laiwu, and making use of Jinan's advantages as a regional financial center, continuing to explore major strategic business chances and innovative development opportunities from reform and transformation, and helping Laishang Bank achieve the new development.

3. To improve the effectiveness of the comprehensive risk management system, by continuing to adhere to the prudent and reliable risk preferences, reasonably setting annual risk tolerance and risk limits in accordance with the economic and financial environment and the actual situations of the whole Bank, and forming a clear and definite risk preference governance framework; improving the comprehensive risk management system, accelerating the construction of the risk monitoring system, properly managing liquidity and market risks, and meeting the regulatory requirements of the Macro-Prudential Assessment (MPA) system, the Liquidity Coverage Rate (LCR), and so on; establishing a long-term management and control mechanism for asset quality, and strengthening the management of loan classifications, provisions and write-offs, to ensure that all asset quality

indicators continue to improve.

4. To continuously improve the capability for capital management, by continuing to use appropriate capital tools, expand capital supplement channels, and optimize the capital structure according to the needs of business development; enhancing capital use efficiency while strengthening dynamic capital management, to ensure the balanced development of quality, efficiency and scale of the Company; paying close attention to the policy dynamics of non-fixed term capital bonds, timely launching the supplement of Tier-1 capital, rationally planning the use of capital and restraining the consumption of capital at the same time, guiding operating institutions to tap their internal potentials, seeking for capital saving space, and achieving the balance between capital and income.

5. To vigorously promote the protection of consumers' rights and interests, by formulating the strategy, policies and goals to protect consumers' rights and interests, continuously improving the intuitive system of protecting consumers' rights and interests, including the protection of consumers' rights and interests into the corporate governance, business goals, and enterprise culture construction, making proper information disclosure of relevant work, ceaselessly tracking, supervising, and evaluating the comprehensive, timeliness, and effectiveness of the protection work of consumers' rights and interests, hearing to special annual report on consumer protection work on a regular basis, exploring the evaluation system for the protection work of consumers' interests and rights, and reasonably applying the assessment results.

6. To continuously improve the team building. Enhancing talent introduction management, introducing some experienced management and professional technical personnel through all kinds of channels, building more incentive salary management system that restrains employees more efficiently, so as to provide talent guarantee for business development of the whole bank. Planning as a whole, educating and training employees by different levels, strengthening talent echelon building, and focusing on promoting a targeted and practical internal training; training middle and high-level management while refining the organization and assessment of training, so as to improve the actual effect of training. Improving management of the teams of reserved cadres and internal trainers, building and improving dynamic assessment management mechanism which will not be affected seriously by new employees and resignation of previous employees, so as to improve the overall quality of the internal trainer team.

7. To focus on improving the brand image, by strengthening the organic combination of hard advertisement and soft media, enhancing the application of new media publicity, and broadening the publicity channels; actively strengthening the cooperation and communication with the information monitoring platforms of public opinions, ensuring the both promotion in positive information publicity and prevention and control of negative public opinions, increasing the efforts to prevent reputation risks; actively fulfilling the social responsibility, continuing to promote green credit, inclusive finance and other policies and measures, actively promoting the transformation of old growth drivers into new ones, and supporting the development of the real economy; continuing

to participate in public welfare undertakings, systematically and continuously carrying out public welfare activities by integrating internal and external resources, and actively repaying the society.

## V. Profit distribution plan

In 2018, the surplus accumulation fund totaled RMB 46,310,359.17; other surplus reserve so accrued was RMB 9,262,071.83; appropriation to general reserve for risk assets was RMB 304,485,251.10; no cash dividend was carried out temporally; and the remaining undistributed profit of RMB 106,747,427.01 shall be carried over to the next year.
