### 二、监事会就有关事项发表的独立意见

### （一）公司依法经营情况

报告期内，公司坚持依法、合规经营，规范管理，经营决策程序符合法律、法规及公司章程的有关规定，董事及高级管理人员在业务经营及管理过程中谨慎、认真、勤勉，未发现违法违规违章行为和损害公司、股东利益行为。

### （二）财务报告真实性

2017年度财务报告已由中兴华会计师事务所根据《企业会计制度》和《企业会计准则》有关规定进行审计，并出具标准无保留意见审计报告。财务报告真实、客观、公允地反映了公司的财务状况和经营成果。

### （三）关联交易情况

公司的关联交易主要是股东贷款。监事会认为，报告期内，公司在处理关联交易时，严格遵守有关规定，履行关联交易操作程序，交易过程体现了公平合理原则，关联交易符合商业原则，未发现损害股东权益和公司利益的行为。

### （四）内部控制制度情况

监事会听取和审阅了《公司2017年度内部控制评价报告》，认为公司制定了较为完整、合理、有效的内部控制制度。

### （五）股东大会决议执行情况

监事会对董事会提交股东大会审议的各项报告和提案进行了查阅和调研，没有异议。监事会对股东大会决议的执行情况进行了监督，认为董事会按照公司治理要求，认真执行了股东大会的有关决议。

### Chapter VIII Report of the Board of Supervisors

## I. Meetings held by the Board of Supervisors in 2018

The Company held four meetings of the Board of supervisors in 2018, with the details as follows:

(I) The 9th Meeting of the 4th Board of Supervisors was held on March 29, 2018, and the following proposals were deliberated and approved, including the 2017 Annual Work Report of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Operation Work Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Financial Report and Profit Distribution Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on 2018 Annual Budget of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on 2018 Annual IT Budget of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Report on IT Work and Plan Execution of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Report on Internal Audit of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Report on Internal Control evaluation of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Report on Safeguarding Consumers' Rights and Interests of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Report on Executive Condition of Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Reporting the Registration Business to the Board of Directors of Q4 2017, Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Extension for Laiwu Steel Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises, Proposal on Comprehensive Credit Extension for Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd. and Its Affiliated Enterprises, Proposal on Pledge of Equity Held in Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. by Tai'an Financial Holding Group Co., Ltd., Proposal on Information Disclosure for '2017 Annual Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.' Evaluation Report on 2017 Annual Duty Performance of Directors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Evaluation Report on 2017 Annual Duty Performance of Supervisors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Evaluation Report on 2017 Annual Duty Performance of Senior Managers by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Comprehensive Risk Management Policies of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Further Standardizing Remuneration of Senior Executives of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Adjusting Composition of Members of All Special Committees of the 4th Board of Supervisors, and Proposal on Holding General Meeting of Shareholders of 2017 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

(II) The 10th Meeting of the 4th Board of Supervisors was held on June 28, 2018, and the following proposals were delivered and approved, including the Operating Work Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q1 2018, Management Measures for Risk Preference of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2018 Annual Strategy for Risk Preference of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., 2017 Annual

Evaluation Report on Internal Capital Adequacy of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Report on Executive Conditions of Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q1 2018, Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q1 2018, and Proposal on Reporting Registration Business to the Board of Directors of Q1 2018.

(III) The 11th Meeting of the 4th Board of Supervisors was held on August 14, 2018, and the following proposals were delivered and approved, including the Operating Work Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of January to June 2018, Proposal on the Equity Management Measures of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., Proposal on Transferring Equity Held in Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. by Laiwu Municipal Finance Bureau to Laiwu Municipal Economic Development & Investment Co., Ltd., Proposal on Pledge of Equity Held in Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. by Heze Urban Construction Engineering Development Group Co., Ltd., Report on Executive Conditions of Related Transactions by Insiders and Shareholders of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q2 2018, Risk Management Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of Q2 2018, Proposal on Registration of Credit Extension Business to the Board of Directors of Q2 2018, proposal on write-off of Non-performing Loans of 2018, IT Work Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. of January to June 2018, and Proposal on Nominating Supervisors of the 4th Board of Supervisors.

(IV) The 12th Meeting of the 4th Board of Supervisors was held on November 29, 2018, and the following proposals were delivered and approved, including the Proposal on Transferring Equity Held in Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. by Panda Financial Holding Corp., Ltd. to Guangyang Antai Holding Co., Ltd., and Proposal on Centralized Disposal and Settlement of partial Non-Performing Assets.

## II. Independent opinion on relevant issues from the Board of Supervisors

#### (i) Legal operation of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company has stuck to operation in accordance with laws and regulations and normalization of the management. The decision-making procedure met the related provisions of laws, regulations and the Articles of Association. The directors and the senior management were able to conscientiously implement their duties. No director or senior management has been discovered to have breached any laws or regulations or Articles of Association or harmed the interest of the Company when they execute their functions.

##### (ii) Authenticity of financial reports

Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP has carefully audited the 2017 Annual Financial Report in accordance with Accounting System for Enterprises and Accounting Standard for Enterprises and issued standard unqualified audit report. The financial report has truthfully and fairly reflected the Company's financial position and operation results.

##### (iii) The related transactions

The related transactions of the Company are mainly shareholders' loans. The Board of

Supervisors think that during the reporting period when dealing with related transactions, the Company has strictly observed related regulations and performed the operating procedure of related transactions. Transaction processes have reflected the principle of fairness and justifiableness. The related transactions meet the commercial principles and no action compromising the interests of the Company or shareholders has been discovered.

##### (iv) The internal control system

The Board of Supervisors heard and deliberated the 2017 Annual Internal Control Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and believed that the Company has formulated the relatively complete, reasonable and effective internal control system.

## (v) Implementation of the resolutions approved in the shareholders' general meetings

The Board of Supervisors has reviewed and investigated all reports and proposals submitted by the Board of Directors to the Shareholders' General Meeting for deliberation, without any objection. The Board of Supervisors has supervised over the implementation of the resolutions approved in the shareholders' general meetings, and thinks that the Board of Directors of the Company is able to conscientiously implement the related resolutions of the shareholders' general meetings according to the requirements of corporate governance.

公司关联交易公平合理，贷款审批程序按照公司现有的信贷业务审批和管理制度执行，贷款利率参照公司利率定价模型进行测算，并遵循市场化原则执行，不存在损害股东权益及公司利益的情况。公司关联方经营状况和财务状况良好，能够履行与公司达成的各项协议，报告期内公司关联交易未产生不良资产。

At the end of the reporting period, the maximum credit extension balance for a single client was RMB 390,000,000, accounting for 3.63% of the total net capital of the Bank; that for a single associated legal person or group client to which the other organization belongs was RMB 1,570,000,000, accounting for 14.59% of the total net capital of the Bank; the total credit extension balance for all affiliated parites was RMB 1,705,000,000, accounting for 15.85% of the total net capital of the Bank, all of which met the regulatory requirements.

The related transactions of the Company are fair and reasonable. The loan examination and approval procedure is carried out according to the current credit business examination and approval and management system of the Company. The loan interest rate is estimated according to the Company's interest rate pricing model, and implemented in line with the principle of being market-oriented, without damage to shareholders' rights and interests or the Company's interests. The operation and financial position of all related parties of the Company are good and able to perform all agreements with the Company. No non-performing asset of related transactions has been incurred during the reporting period.
